    190: function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
    191:   if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
    192:   if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
    193:   if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
    194:   if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
    195:   if(typeof value==='object'){
    196:     const out={};
    197:     for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
    198:       if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
    199:       out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    200:     }
    201:     return out;
    202:   }
    203:   return String(value);
    204: }

    490: async function ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify=true}={}){
    491:   uepDiagRecord('session.ensure.start',{verify,online:navigator.onLine});

    502:   const email=String(saved?.email||'').trim();
    503:   uepDiagRecord('session.identity.available',{hasName:Boolean(name),hasEmail:Boolean(email),source:state?.auth?.user?'auth.user':state?.auth?.rememberedUser?'auth.rememberedUser':'settings.userProfile'});

   7800:   if(!match)return "";

   8098: function dashboardAdmissionMethod(row){
   8099:   const text=[row['선발방식'],row['핵심평가'],row['주요평가자료'],row['한줄요약']].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
   8100:   if(row['선발방식'])return String(row['선발방식']);
   8101:   const out=[];if(/교과|내신/.test(text))out.push('내신');if(/수능최저|최저/.test(text))out.push('수능최저');if(/서류|학생부/.test(text))out.push('서류');if(/면접/.test(text))out.push('면접');if(/논술/.test(text))out.unshift('논술');if(/수능/.test(text)&&/정시|수능/.test(String(row['전형유형']||'')))return '수능100 또는 영역별 반영';return [...new Set(out)].join(' + ')||'대학별 방식 확인';

    634: async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
    635:   if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
    636:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
    637:   url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
    638:   url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
    639:   url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
    640:   ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
    641:   const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
    642:   if (!response.ok) {
    643:     let detail = "";
    644:     try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    645:     if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
    646:       const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
    647:       const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
    648:       await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
    649:       return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    650:     }
    651:     throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
    652:   }
--- around line 635 ---
    625:     headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    626:     body,
    627:   });
    628:   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
    629:   const payload = await response.json();
    630:   if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
    631:   return payload.access_token;
    632: }

    634: async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
    635:   if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
    636:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
    637:   url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
    638:   url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
    639:   url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
    640:   ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
    641:   const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
    642:   if (!response.ok) {
    643:     let detail = "";
    644:     try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    645:     if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
    646:       const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
    647:       const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
    648:       await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
    649:       return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    650:     }
    651:     throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
    652:   }
    653:   return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
    654: }
